Posts for 2018-07

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Jul 01 2018 02:00:35 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

To get votes, Modiji and RSS-workers first said that they will impose ban against conversion. Now after getting bribes from Missionaries, RSS-workers and Modiji REMOVE the 40 year old ban on conversion in Arunachal Pradesh !!!
.
I at Right to Rexcall Party had proposed following law-drafts to reduce Missionaries rising dominance
.
(1) Right to Recall District Education Officer - this will improve education and so conversion will reduce
.
(2) Right to Recall District Medical Officer - this will improve health care in Govt hospitals and so conversion will reduce
.
(3) Cancellation of patent laws and restoring 1971 patent law --- to reduce prices of medicine
.
(4) reduction in use of chemicals in farming -- to reduce cancer etc
.
(5) Wealth tax on ALL religious institution MINUS Rs 1000 per year per follower that that sect has
.
and so on.
.
RSS-workers opposed all these law-drafts. Instead, in may-2014 election, they promised that they would print a law to end conversion and this end conversion problem.
.
Its now clear that all this promise of anti-conversion law was ONLY too fool Hindus and get votes..
.
Now here is what Modiji and RSS-workers are doing in Arunachal Pradesh --- they are repealing a 40 year old law (made by congress), that prohibits conversion !!!
.
So I had opposed conversion-banning-laws as useless proposals. So one may say that "my thinking is same as PRESENT day think of RSS-workers". But the question --- why did RSS-workers from 1950 till 2014 proposed such bans?
.
Suffices to say --- Modiji and RSS-workers have no convictions and no commitment to anything. For votes they will say one thing, and when they come into position, and get bribes, they have NO problems in doing just the opposite.
.
So I request all those who want to reduce influence of Missionaries should dismiss Modiji and RSS-workers as psuedo-solutions. Imo, they should instead promote law-drafts such as RTR-Deo etc
.
Of the 5 law-drafts I mentioned abive, we have uploaded two on newindia dot in. So what action can you take TODAY to reduce conversion? Pls study Rtr-Deo draft given at https://www.facebook.com/mehtarahulc/posts/10155139313526922 . If you support this draft, then pls vote at https://newindia.in/causes/RtrDeo3

  • https://newindia.in/causes/RtrDeo3
  • https://external-hou1-1.xx.fbcdn.net/emg1/v/t13/5928470435598452773?url=https%3A%2F%2Fnewindia.in%2Fwp-content%2Fuploads%2F2017%2F08%2FVote.jpg&fb_obo=1&utld=newindia.in&_nc_sid=632586&_nc_ht=external-hou1-1.xx.fbcdn.net&stp=c17.0.1556.2407a_dst-jpg_flffffff_q75_s139x215_tt6&ccb=18-1&_nc_gid=_DXcd7NVo64Hkn11FcORQA&_nc_zt=3&oh=06_Q3_DATUQSIs-hBX_CeS3-DjZtE_rRh_BzaqRAvCp03dOo-4M&oe=6AC2A4F5

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Jul 01 2018 02:21:20 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

(B018) Proposed law-drafts to reduce influence of Missionaries
.
To fool Hindus and collect Hindu votes, some self certified "hinduvaadies": and "nationalist" political groups like RSS promise anti-conversion laws. The laws they have printed in Gujarat is a royal piece of nonsense. And, CM in Arunachal Pradesh from same RSS in jun-2018 demand ending a law that bans conversion !!!
.
Imo, one cant tie people with trees.
.
I have proposed following law-drafts to reduce conversions in India
.
(1) Right to Recall District Education Officer - this will improve education and so conversion will reduce
.
(2) Right to Recall District Medical Officer - this will improve health care in Govt hospitals and so conversion will reduce
.
(3) Cancellation of patent laws and restoring 1971 patent law --- to reduce prices of medicine
.
(4) reduction in use of chemicals in farming -- to reduce cancer etc
.
(5) Wealth tax on ALL religious institution MINUS Rs 1000 per year per follower that that sect has
.
and so on.
.
This album will have list of all laws which imo can reduce rising influence of Missionaries in India and reduce conversions.
.
And as an important point, I request you to note that real strength to Missionaries is coming from FDI and huge amount of plots they own. So any person who is pro-FDI and against wealth tax, is defacto pro-missionaries, So while SoGa / Arke and congress/aap are OPENLY pro-missionaries, Modiji and RSS are psuedo-anti-missionaries only.

.

.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Jul 01 2018 05:34:06 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

One more way to send message to PmoIndia , which cant be faked
.
One can send contribution of Rs 102 exact to (PM relief fund) using BHIM, and send 40 character long message. The message can be links to newindia dot in , which has the law-drafts you want.
.
Now BHIM is linked with aadhar, So fake messages can NOT be sent.
.
Using RTI, one may be able to obtain list of all messages sent to PM relief fund where donation amount was Rs 102.
.
The recipet can be kept on phone or uploaded on FB as proof.
.
Currently, one cant send amount below Rs 100. .
.
So this method cant be used for all 1000 law-drafts that a person supports, But he can surely use it for 1 to 10 law-drafts he supports most.
.
Also, one can send at most 40 characters. So he can send 5 to 8 hashtags of newindia dot in labels he supports.

  • https://scontent-hou1-1.xx.fbcdn.net/v/t1.6435-9/36394578_10155392507581922_8466612882497339392_n.jpg?stp=dst-jpg_tt6&cstp=mx280x497&ctp=s280x497&_nc_cat=104&ccb=1-7&_nc_sid=f727a1&_nc_ohc=uOgsaKZmyJ8Q7kNvwFRmUDl&_nc_oc=AdojKJThaYQAKBDhFJLYZU5wXlLX97fHrJZ6pLVTu_M0Q9NR2zslNwKjFzOjC4tgRkI&_nc_zt=23&_nc_ht=scontent-hou1-1.xx&_nc_gid=q8Fo45AAQdWujDF_JPT3-Q&_nc_ss=7a289&oh=00_AQOlz9JLIMXXRyzjFl94uWHwjf6Dq_OPq3T6uP73tzhyBg&oe=6AE83ED3

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Jul 01 2018 12:11:02 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

मन्दसौर रेप केस
.
समाधान — मंदसौर जिले की मतदाता सूची में से 25-55 वर्ष तक की आयु के बीच के 50 नागरिकों की ज्यूरी बुलाई जाए । यह ज्यूरी इस मामले की नियमित सुनवाई करके आरोपी को सज़ा देगी । ज्यूरी चाहे तो आरोपी का सार्वजनिक नार्को टेस्ट लेकर यह पता लगा सकती है कि क्या आरोपी पूर्व में भी इस तरह के यौन अपराध में लिप्त रह चुके है । ज्यूरी आरोप सिद्ध होने पर अभियुक्तों को फाँसी दे सकती है ।
.
इस तरह इस मामले का निपटान हफ़्ते दो हफ़्ते में हो जाएगा , और कठोर सज़ा मिलने से अन्य अपराधी हतोत्साहित होंगे ।
.
ज्यूरी के गठन एवं संचालन से सम्बंधित प्रस्तावित प्रक्रिया का ड्राफ़्ट मेरी फ़ेसबुक प्रोफ़ायल के कवर पेज को क्लिक करके देखा जा सकता है ।
.
सोनिया-केजरीवाल एवं इनके अंध भगत आरोपीयों की ज्यूरी द्वारा सुनवाई किए जाने का विरोध कर रहे है । लेकिन उनका यह विरोध महत्त्वहीन है । क्योंकि यदि मोदी साहेब चाहे तो ज्यूरी ट्रायल का आदेश कर सकते है । लेकिन संघ के सभी कार्यकर्ता एवं मोदी साहेब नही चाहते कि अभियुक्तों को त्वरित रूप से कठोर सज़ा मिले । वे इसका साम्प्रदायिक इस्तेमाल करके भावनाएँ भड़काना चाहते है । क्योंकि इससे संघ का वोट बैंक बढ़ता है । इसीलिए संघ के कार्यकर्ता एवं मोदी साहेब भी अभियुक्तों के सार्वजनिक नार्को टेस्ट एवं ज्यूरी ट्रायल का विरोध कर रहे है ।

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Jul 01 2018 16:29:22 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Useless entity called as "Constitution of Political Party"
.
Many people ask me about Constitution of Right to Recall Party, that I am working to get registered. My reply is --- Constitution of a political party is a nonsense concept as of today in India. I made a constitution because election commission has a nonsense rule that a political party which needs registration MUST have a constitution, And then I give them two links --- first is constitution that I will give to ECI and another is the real document which has rules I will implement. (pls see comment for links)
.
Now why is Constitution of political party useless?
.
A rule is useful if govt implements it. Today, no court and no govt and even ECI doesnt refer to Party Constitutions when members of party have dispute. There is NO law in PRA which gives primacy to party constitution.
.
The most important issue in political party is --- who will get loksabha tickets. The ECI goes by list signed by President of the party !! ECI doesnt even see if the list was made by the process mentioned in constitution. IOW, most important issue is settled by signature of one person and no process is DEFACTO required to be followed.
.
Next --- ECI says that party officers bears must be elected by party members democratically. But ECI puts NO restriction on how LS candidates should be appointed by party !! IOW, ECI focuses on issue of petty appointments and ignore main issues
.
IOW, as of now, ECI allows parties to be run by discretion of party's president or apex body of 2-5 leaders and thats all.
.
IOW, ECI never enforces constitution of a political party on a party, But ECI has long list of good things that must be present in the party constitution at the time of registration !!
.
In cases of companies, Company Law has long list of guidelines that companies must follow. ECI guidelines are all "suggestive" and not binding. They become binding ONLY when polls come, and these binding guidelines are only wrt election and not how parties are to be run.
.
So finally, why have two constitutions? I will reply this after party registration is over.

.

.

.
This is also possible in public limited companies, where shared are openly traded. Here, newcomers and outsiders can take control. But here, newcomers will have BUY shares and share prices will increase as more shares are bought. IOW, newcomers will be able to buy company and control over it, but they will need to pay a huge price to existing shareholders first. No such mechanism is possible within ECI guidelines.
.
Suffices to say, ECI has put pretty nonsensical guidelines about what party constitution must have.
.

.
ECI leaves them with only one choice. Write the constitution as ECI wants, and then make changes that founders want !!! Or , party apex can simply ignore the party Constitution !!!

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Wed Jul 04 2018 17:46:44 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

How GST will reduce employee efficiency in large company as well, and how RERA will further reduce efficiency of even large builders
.
I will first give example of one large builder I happened to know. He had staff over 100 civil engineers, along with 10s of other professions. One remark he made, which all business owners will see --- (a) some of his civil engineers were 9 am to 5 pm , just do work assigned to them and never take much deep interest even in their own work (b) but some 10% of his engineers were hyperactive --- they would do not just their work perfectly, but would also be always willing to assist others work such as plumbing, electricity, tiles fitting, chasing labor and almost everything, even assisting CAs !!
.
Not only these hyperactive staff was prompt, perfectionist, always trying to ensure that their and others' work is error free, but they were putting more hours without asking. And their total output would be several times normal, thought salary would be same or sometime may be just 20% to 30% more.

What makes such hyperactive employees tick?
.
In almost all cases, their goals was --- to start their own business !! To start their own construction firm or contractor.
.
So they were willing to learn ALL disciplines' and all agencies' works because their goal was to become builders themselves. So they would try their best to get experience in everything. So these employees were not just productive, but they would also increase others' productivity. And they become benchmark for other employees to perform.
.
One can see this across industries, across countries across time. In software business, an engineer is more motivated than in civil engineering. Because in software, starting new business is easier. In automobile engineering, the engineers are far less motivated than civil engineering, because starting own motor making company is far far more difficult than becoming a builder.
.
But now say that Govt rules become so, that it is almost impossible for a newcomer to start business. In such cases, such hyperactive employees would see that there is no way they can become master of their own business. So many of them will eventually start behaving like mediocres as well.
.
Thats what GST does, and RERA does worse
.
GST is making MORE difficult for newcomer to start business. Not just CA costs and accounting headaches have increased, but buyers insist on buying only from large sellers due to "buyer will bear burden of missing trader" clause i.e. clause-16.2.c of CGST act . So newcomer doesnt get clients easily.
.
So now newcomers are finding it more and more difficult to start manufacturing business. And so they have no option but to remain employee. Even the active ones see that they will never be able to run their own business, and will remain as employee for whole life. And so their motivation will start decreasing.
.
And RERA is killing existing small builders, and making it far more difficult for new small builders to come up.
.
So all these factors will further reduce active employees' enthusism even in large companies. And so over productivity and INNOVATION will decrease
.
And meanwhile USA has no excise, no GST, no VAT, no service tax and has only state level sales tax on end sales of goods (and not on services). And they have less RERA like laws. So motivation of employees will be higher in US companies in USA> So they will be able to accomplish more innovation and higher productivity. And so US companies will become more competitive than Indian companies,. and will be able to defeat Indian companies in almost all fields
.
SOLUTION?
.
Solution I propose is to scrap GST and instead implement wealth tax. And RERA should be replaced by Jury System, where Jurors can punish an errant builder, but not impose suffocating restrictions on him.
.
If you support wealth tax, pls vote at https://newindia.in/causes/WealthTax . (newindia is central govt website , which ministers may use to know public opinion). . To see wealth tax draft summary, pls see --- https://www.facebook.com/mehtarahulc/posts/10154809049286922 .
.
And pls note that in India, SoMoke have always opposed wealth tax and always oppose JurySys. And all congress/rss/aap workers also oppose wealth tax and oppose JurySys. So if you support wealth tax over GST, then pls inform as many as you can that SoMoke and congress/rss/aap workers all oppose wealth tax, JurySys etc
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Jul 08 2018 04:12:52 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

#LetHotelUseIGST. - Allow hotel customers to pay IGST instead of SGST.
.
If you support following proposal, then pls vote at
https://newindia.in/causes/LetHotelUseIGST
.
And pls also tweet "@PmoIndia #LetHotelUseIGST . Pls allow hotel customers to pay IGST instead of SGST so that out of state service providers' services dont get doubly taxed . pls see https://newindia.in/causes/LetHotelUseIGST "
.
Pls see my tweetlink in first comment
.
=========
---------------------------------
Allow hotel customers to pay IGST instead of SGST
----------------------------------
.
Respected Finance Minister,
Govt of India,
New Delhi.
.
sub : allow a hotel to issue IGST bill instead of SGST, if the customer has registration in a different state
.
(1) Say a service provider from state-A provides services in state-B, and has to stay in a hotel in state-B. In such case, hotel will give bill with 9% SGST (or 14% SGST) of state-B, which the service provider coming from state-A can NOT defacto use as input credit.
.
(2) So now please consider situation of a small service provider vs a large service provide.
.
(2a) The large service provider will have business across India in almost all states, and so will have registration in all states. While the small service provider will have registration in only one state or two states
.
(2b) So a large service provider will be able to use hotel’s SGST as input credit. But a small business provider will NOT be able to use SGST of other states, because small service provider has registration only in one state
.
(2c) so defacto, this causes DOUBLE TAXATION on services of small service provider. And makes small service provider LESS competitive. So customers may have to prefer a large service provider even when small service provider is more efficient. This is a loss to India’s competitiveness in economy.
.
(3) SOLUTION : we request you to make changes in GST laws, so that hotel may issue IGST bill when customer is from other state.

  • https://newindia.in/causes/LetHotelUseIGST

राजपत्र (गैजेट) क्या है व इसमें कितनी शक्ति है ?
.
राजपत्र (गैजेट) की शक्ति बहुत अधिक है 8 तारीख को अंधभक्तों के प्रभु मोदी जी 8 बजे एक भाषण देते हैं और सुबह तक देश को सड़क पर ला देते हैं ये हम सबने देखा लेकिन ये अर्धसत्य है क्योंकि कुछ दिन बाद एक भाषण अरुण जेटली झाड़ते हैं कि अब शादी वाले परिवार बैंक से ढाई लाख करारे व नए नोट ले सकते हैं लेकिन इस बात पर किसी भी प्रशासनिक अधिकारी ने अमल नहीं किया (वो बात अलग है कि अब भी गैर जरूरी शर्तों के कारण शादी वाले परिवार को पैसे नहीं मिल रहे)
.
अब प्रश्न ये उठता है कि मोदी जी के भाषण ने तो हंगामा मचा दिया अरुण जेटली के भाषण को किसी ने क्यों नहीं माना?
.
इसका कारण ये है कि मोदी ने भाषण बाद में पढ़ा और राजपत्र (गैजेट) में पहले हस्ताक्षर किये जबकि अरुण जेटली जी केवल जुमला छोड़कर चले गए यदि कल प्रधानमंत्री जी राजपत्र (गैजेट) में ये छाप दें कि सभी भारतियों के खाते में 10000 हज़ार रूपये डाल दें तो प्रशासन सभी भारतीय के खाते में 10000 रूपये डाल देगा उसके लिए प्रधानमंत्री जी को टीवी पर आकर नौटंकी करने की आवश्यकता नहीं पड़ेगी।
.
दूसरी और यदि प्रधानमंत्री जी टीवी पर आकर ये बात रोज बोलते रहते है तो भी किसी के बैंक खाते में चवन्नी भी नहीं आएगी राजपत्र (गैजेट) प्रशासन के लिए सीधा भगवान का आदेश होता है जो आपने उपरोक्त उदाहरणों में समझ लिया होगा।
.
मैं सभी कार्यकर्ताओं से निवेदन करूँगा की वे भाषणबाजी पर बिलकुल ध्यान ना दें क्योंकि हमारे नेताओं ने तो इसमे पीएचडी की हुई है व भाषणबाजी में प्रधानमंत्री जी तो एक अच्छे अभिनेता भी हैं वो एक अच्छे ब्रांड एम्बेसडर भी हैं कभी JIO की कभी कैशलेस की ब्रान्डिंग करते नजर आते रहते हैं यदि आप देश में कुछ अच्छे बदलाव चाहते हैं तो ये सोचें कि राजपत्र (गैजेट) में क्या छपवाना है।
अधिकतर अच्छे बदलाव तो राजपत्र (गैजेट) में छपने से ही आ जाएंगे इसके लिए सदन में बहुमत की आवश्यकता नहीं होगी कुछ अच्छे कानूनों के ड्राफ्ट के लिंक नीचे दे रहा हूँ जिनके राजपत्र (गैजेट) में छपने से देश सही दिशा में जाएगा।
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Tue Jul 10 2018 14:44:44 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

steps you (and I) suggest to communicate to PMO --- VERY IMPORTANT
.
Dear all recallist - THIS POST IS VERY IMPORTANT
.
table of contents
.
(1) steps you mention voters to communicate to PMO
(2) what this question does NOT include
(3) my answer
(4) further detailing of the question
(5) why communicating to PMO is necessary?.
.
====================
.
chap-1 : steps to voters/activists to communicate to PMO
.. . . . -------------------------------------------------
.
There 90 crore voters in India. And say there are 2 lakh activists.
.
Each activist / voter may be supporting 0 to 10 to 100 to 1000 law-drafts. And imo, each voter / activist must communicate to PMO which law-drafts he wants. The voter may also need to communicate to MPs, CM, MLA, Mayor, other activists, other voters etc, But communicating to PM imo is a MUST. Also, pls note that "I communicated to MP and so communicating to PM is not needed" is imo a wrong answer. The voter must try to communicate to PM first and communicating to MP too may be needed.
.
So pls mention exact steps that YOU suggest 2 lakh activists and crores of voters to take to communicate to PM the 5 to 500 law-drafts that those activists / voters may be supporting. .
.
Pls write steps in language that 12th pass person (assuming he knows language you use) can understand/
.
============================
.
chap-2 : What this question does NOT include
. . . . . . .-------------------------------------------------
.
And pls note --- the steps I am asking for here are to "COMMUNICATING to PM" . NOT communicating MPs, or communicating to people who know you, people whom you know, friends, newcomers etc.
.
Also, communication should be direct. So a method that "I told activist-A to communicate to PM" here is not an answer here
.
So following are separate questions
.
(2.a) communicating to MPs about law-drafts you support
.
(2.b) communicating to people who know you
.
(2.c) communicate to people who know you and people whom you know
.
(2.d) putting information about law-drafts you want in a format that new people --- whom you will come to know you in future --- can obtain the information by getting just 1-2 links from you
.
(2.e) putting information about law-drafts you want in a format that new people --- whom you will come to know you in future and whom you will come to know in future --- obtain the information by getting just 1-2 links from you
.
This status is ONLY about what steps do you suggest to 2 lakh activists and 90 crore voters, TO INFORM PM about 0 to 1000 law-drafts that they all support..
.
==============================
.
chap-3 : steps I suggest to communicate to PMO
. . .-------------------------------------------------
.
Now I will give my answer, and then further elaborate the question
.
The steps I suggest to 20 lakh activists and 90 crore voters to COMMUNICATE TO PM are as follows
.
(3.a) I request each activist / voter to send postcard to PM with exact label of the law-draft. The label must be the label used on newIndia dot in . The sender must put his voterid / name / address on postcard (signature optional).
.
(3.b) vote at the newindia dot in link where the law-draft is uploaded
.
(3.c) tweet " @PmoIndia #newindia_label #newindia_link " where label is same as label or title used in newindia dot in
.
(4) optional - also sign petition on change dot org, with if possible, same label as newindia dot in. and send one more tweet "" @PmoIndia #change_org_label #change_org_link "
.
Then there are many many more steps. About above 3-4 steps are main.
.
=============
.
chap-4 : Further elaboration of question
. . . . . -------------------------------------------------
.
Now amongst 90 crore voters,
(a) some 5 crore are skilled enough to use twitter
(b) some 20 crore can use OTP based websites but NOT twitter
(c) some 70 crore can neither use twitter not OTP etc
.
So you must give methods that ALL three may use. And you may also give methods that more skilled voters can use
.
And amongst 2 lakh activists also, some wont know how to use twitter, but most would know. And you may assume that almost all know how to use OTP based websites. And you may assume that some, but not most, know excel etc.
.
So some of the basic steps you mention must be applicable to all 90 crore voters. And then you may give some steps that only some voters may use.

=====================
.
(5) Why communicating to PMO is necessary?
.
Many laws we have proposed dont need MPs' approval and PM's signature will suffice. eg MRCM, RTR-PM, one version of RTR-MP, RTR-SCCj, RTR-SCj, RTR-HCCJ etc. And even if MP's approval is needed, PM can always print the law as Ordinance and then ask MP to approve or reject.
.
And "should I communicate to PM or not", is subjective.
.
eg if X says that I support law-draft-A, then first request I will make is "did you tell PmoIndia that you want law-draft-A"? And after several reminders, if X refuses to communicate to PmoIndia, then I will NOT take X as supporter of law-draft-A. He may or or may nor be, But I wont be spending my time with him. After all, my time is important (and so is everyone's time). And no activist-A would like to wok with an voter-B or activist-B if voter-B or activist-B doesnt meet certain criteria. And one NECESSARY condition is communicating to PMO in a manner that doesnt disrupt civil life of other citizens..
.
So imo, steps to communicae PM are important.
.
Now I have given ways I request all 90 crore voters and 2 lakh activists to communicate to PmoIndia, that they support A, B, or C law-drafts. The ball is in YOUR court,. On your wall, in some designated album, pls write a FB status where YOU specify which steps YOU are asking 2 lakh activists and 80 crore voters to take to communicate to PmoIndia that they support A001, A002 to A500 law-drafts

Verifying whether X has sent postcard to PM or not demanding law-draft-A
.
Consider a law-draft namely https://newindia.in/Ddmrcam .
.
If a person-A says that he supports MRCM, then first question I will ask him is "did you send postcard to PM citing '#Ddmrcam https://newindia.in/Ddmrcam ' with your voterid / name ? If not, you may be a supporter or may not be, but I will not take you as DdMracam supporter, and I dont want to spend time wrt to activism for DdMrcam with you".
.
Now say he says he did send this postcard. Then how would I know that he is speaking the truth?
.
Solution is --- I will give him blank postcard and ask him to write again, and send that postcard myself !!!
.
If a person has send the postcard once, hen he should have no problem in sending it again.
.
So when activist-A meets activist-B, A can ask for list of causes B has supported by sending postcard to PM. From those 10-20-100 causes, A can pick any 5, and ask B to write 5 postcards with those 5 causes again !! If B writes, then A can send those postcards again, and so now A is convinced. And B may or need not do the same process for 1 to 5 causes that A supports. That way, A may verify B or B may verify A or both may verify each other.
.
------------
.
How is verification useful?
.
These days, I run into too many people who claim that they support RTR !! How can I convince neutral audience that they are fake? Well, I can ask the question "did you ask PmoIndia to print which RTR-drafts"? They give no clear answer and that convinces the audiences that they are fake. And if they say "RTR-PM-law-draft" or some such RTR-law-draft, then my next question is "pls tell me how you communicated to Pmoindia to print this Rtr-X-law-draft you claim you support". And then finally, if they say they did write a postcard, then by asking him again, I can either increase support for Rtr-X-law-draft, or I can convince audience. At least one good purpose is served !!

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Wed Jul 11 2018 03:59:56 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Possibility of replacing income tax by proposed "wealth increase tax" and proposed "wealth tax"
.
I have discussed proposed wealth tax in previous posts and wont discuss here. The new concept, yet undrafted, I am proposing here is "wealth increase tax" and imo that can replace income tax, but not wealth tax
.
And for the purpose of this tax, "Wealth" will exclude money in current accounts and cash in hand !! So all citizens will have a 0% current account, in which he may keep zero balance for no charge. And wealth will include FDs, savings, land etc
.
Now proposed wealth tax on FDs / savings will be zero. But wealth increase tax will apply on FDs / savings , but not cash in hand and current account
.
How does this proposed "wealth increase tax" apply?
.
First, it applies on book value and NOT market value. (note - proposed wealth tax applies on estimated market value, and not book value).
.
Say tax starts on say 01-jan-2018;. Say a man's BOOK VALUE of wealth on 01-jan-2018 is say Rs 1.00 crore
.
Now say his
wealth on 02-jan-2018 is Rs 1.20 crore
no change till 05-jan-2018
wealth on 06-jan-2018 is Rs 1.50 crore
no change till 12-jan-2018
wealth on 12-jan-2018 is Rs 1.10 crore
no change there after for 4 years
.
So he will pay
(a) wealth increase tax on 20 lakhs for 3 days
(b) wealth increase tax on 50 lakhs for 7 days
(c) wealth increase tax on 10 lakhs for next 4 years
.
So say tax rate is 5% a year.
.
So wealth tax increase tax will be

(a) 20 lakhs * 3 / 365 * 5 / 100 = Rs 822
(b) 50 lakhs * 3 / 365 * 5 / 100 = Rs 822
(c) 10 lakhs * 4 * 5 / 100 = Rs 2 lakhs
.
Now for exact definition, I am using wealth owned on daily basis. But for implementation for lower values of wealth, one may use wealth at monthly basis. .
.
The basis --- whatever has been increase in wealth, it will be taxed at say 5% a year, for at most 4 years, but NOT if increase disappears in a smaller time duration.
.
The plus point over income tax is --- one doesnt need to store all and match all transactions while calculating and verifying taxes. One has to show only his books. And unlike income tax, here (wealth increase + wealth decrease) gets taxed at a lower rate. So it will be less damaging for a risk taker.
.
I will put more details when I get some time./

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Wed Jul 11 2018 14:15:05 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

मोदी साहेब का मास्टर स्ट्रोक -- भारत सरकार ने जिओ इंस्टीट्युट को IoE ( IoE = institute of excellence) का दर्जा दिया !!!
.
लेकिन मौजूदा नीति के अनुसार -- निजी कोलेज वगेरह को सरकारी अनुदान नहीं दिए जा सकते। क्योंकि जिओ ने अभी तक अपना बैंक एकाउंट नहीं खुलवाया है !! हम देखेंगे कि "जैसे ही श्याम प्लेटफोर्म पर पहुंचा वैसे ही रेलगाड़ी चल दी" कि तर्ज पर जिओ द्वारा बेंक एकाउंट खुलवाते ही सरकार कि यह नीति बदल जायेगी।
.
एक बार एक व्यक्ति ने किसी संत से पूछा था की -- "यदि ईश्वर मौजूद है तो मुझे वह दिखाई क्यों नहीं देता" ? संत ने जवाव दिया , "दूध खट्टा हुए बिना कितनी देर तक टिका रह सकता है। लगभग एक दिन। लेकिन यदि आप इसका दही जमा दे तो अगले 2-3 दिन तक इसका सेवन किया जा सकता है। बटर बनाने पर यह लगभग सप्ताह भर तक खराब नहीं होगा और यदि आप इससे घी बना लेते है तो इसे सालों तक खाया जा सकता है। तो इस तरह दूध में वर्षो तक टिके रहने का गुण है , लेकिन क्या आप इसे देख सकते है ?
.
तो जिओ के पास आज छात्र नहीं है , शिक्षक नहीं है और यहाँ तक कि इमारत भी नहीं है। लेकिन यह तय है कि आने वाले समय में जिओ के पास एक विशाल इमारत , नोबेल पुरूस्कार प्राप्त महान शिक्षक और अति मेधावी छात्र होंगे। यह विश्वनीय है कि मुकेश भाई भी रोकेफेलर एवं रोथ्स्चाइल्ड कि तरह IoE के लिए नोबेल कमिटी को सेट कर सकते है।
.
तो किसी व्यक्ति में यह काबिलियत होनी चाहिए कि वह एक बीज में पूरा पेड़ देख सके। और ऐसे किसी बीज को बीज न कहकर सीधे पेड़ कह कर संबोधित करने के लिए और भी ज्यादा असाधारण मेधा कि जरूरत होती है। और सौभाग्य से हमारे पास ऐसा प्रधानमंत्री है जो ऐसी ईश्वरीय मेधा से प्लावित है।
.
और मुकेश भाई ने मोदी साहेब को इसके एवज में जो तर्क दिया वह भी एकदम वाजिब था -- "अगर आप ऐसे बी ए का तमगा ले सकते है तो मेरा कोलेज IoE का स्टेट्स क्यों न पा सकता" !!
.
कुल मिलाकर बिना इमारतो, बिना शिक्षको एवं बिना छात्रों के किसी कोलेज के लिए असाधारण संस्थान का दर्जा हासिल कर लेना वाकई में एक असाधारण उपलब्धि है। और यह कहने कि जरूरत नहीं कि ऐसा मास्टर स्ट्रोक सिर्फ मोदी साहेब कि लगा सकते है।
.

तो जिओ के पास आज छात्र नहीं है , शिक्षक नहीं है और यहाँ तक कि इमारत भी नहीं है। लेकिन यह तय है कि आने वाले समय में जिओ के पास एक विशाल इमारत , नोबेल पुरूस्कार प्राप्त महान शिक्षक और अति मेधावी छात्र होंगे। यह विश्वनीय है कि मुकेश भाई भी रोकेफेलर एवं रोथ्स्चाइल्ड कि तरह IoE के लिए नोबेल कमिटी को सेट कर सकते है।
.
तो किसी व्यक्ति में यह काबिलियत होनी चाहिए कि वह एक बीज में पूरा पेड़ देख सके। और ऐसे किसी बीज को बीज न कहकर सीधे पेड़ कह कर संबोधित करने के लिए और भी ज्यादा असाधारण मेधा कि जरूरत होती है। और सौभाग्य से हमारे पास ऐसा प्रधानमंत्री है जो ऐसी ईश्वरीय मेधा से प्लावित है।
.
और मुकेश भाई ने मोदी साहेब को इसके एवज में जो तर्क दिया वह भी एकदम वाजिब था -- "अगर आप ऐसे बी ए का तमगा ले सकते है तो मेरा कोलेज IoE का स्टेट्स क्यों न पा सकता" !!
.
कुल मिलाकर बिना इमारतो, बिना शिक्षको एवं बिना छात्रों के किसी कोलेज के लिए असाधारण संस्थान का दर्जा हासिल कर लेना वाकई में एक असाधारण उपलब्धि है। और यह कहने कि जरूरत नहीं कि ऐसा मास्टर स्ट्रोक सिर्फ मोदी साहेब कि लगा सकते है।

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Thu Jul 12 2018 11:53:37 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

जिओ इंस्टिट्यूट -- मौजूदा नीति के अनुसार अभी जिओ इंस्टीट्यूट को कोई सरकारी अनुदान नहीं मिलेंगे , लेकिन इसे IoE = institute of excellence का दर्जा होने के कारण आने वाले समय में इसे भारी भरकम प्रोजेक्ट के नाम पर सरकार से बड़ी धनराशि मिलेगी।
.
-----------
.
ONGC / GAIL आदि ने तेल की वास्तविक रॉयल्टी से कम चुका कर भारत में बड़े पैमाने पर लूट चलायी और इस घपले से ONGC / GAIL के कर्मचारीयों / मंत्रीयों / जजों ने मोटा पैसा बनाया। ONGC के सामान्य कर्मचारी के पास भी करोड़ो की सम्पत्ति है !! मेरे एक परिचित 2010 में ONGC के शीर्ष अधिकारी के संपर्क में आये थे , और ये अधिकारी तब 300 करोड़ का निवेश करने के लिए प्रॉपर्टी ढूंढ रहे थे !!!
.
तो भारत के सभी सार्वजनिक उपक्रम = PSU संसाधनों को लूटने के अड्डे बने हुए है , और यह लूट मंत्रियो की मिली भगत से चलायी जाती है। और तेल के धंधे में लगे हुए सार्वजनिक उपक्रम इस प्रकार के घपलो के महारथी है।
.
रिलायंस ने भी इस तरह से लूट चलायी। रिलायंस ने मंत्रियो को घूस दी और एवज में मंत्रियो ने उनके लिए रॉयल्टी की सस्ती दरें तय की। और इस तरह रिलायंस ने भारी मुनाफा कमाया। लेकिन रिलायंस के कर्मचारियों के पास घपले करके करोड़ो रूपये बनाने का अवसर नहीं है। हालांकि रिलायंस के शीर्ष अधिकारियों ने इससे हजारो करोड़ रूपये बनाये , किन्तु रिलायंस के मध्यम दर्जे के अधिकारीयों की तुलना में ONGC के कर्मचारी काफी अधिक अमीर है।
.
मुफ्त में लूटे गए इसी पैसे के एक हिस्से का इस्तेमाल जिओ मोबाइल जैसी चैरिटी योजना में किया गया , और जिओ इंस्टीट्यूट भी इसी तरह की चैरिटी योजना है। इस तरह की चैरिटी में व्यक्ति से उसके हिस्से के 100 रूपये नियमो के अधीन लूट लिए जाते है और इसमें से 2 रूपये चैरिटी के रूप में लौटा दिए जाते है। । लेकिन ONGC इस तरह की लूट चलाने के बावजूद इस तरह की कभी कोई चैरिटी नहीं करता। ONGC सिर्फ मंत्रियो एवं कर्मचारियों के लिए ही पैसा बनाता है !!
.
और रिलायंस रोकेफेलर की कम्पनी है। रोकेफेलर सामने से रिलायंस का CEO बनकर इसे नहीं चलाना चाहता, क्योंकि उसे मालूम है कि भारतीयो को गोरो से एलर्जी है। और इसीलिए रोकेफेलर को भारत में कारोबार करने के लिए एक भारतीय चेहरा चाहिए। तो इसीलिए हमारे पास अम्बानी है। 1858 के बाद ब्रिटिश भारतीय राजाओ के माध्यम से ही अपना संचालन करते थे। यह वही स्थिति है।
.
कहानी का सबक यह है कि -- जब तक भारत में ONGC जैसी संस्थाएं लूट चलाती रहेगी तब तक वे ऐसी निजी कंपनियों को खड़ा रखेंगे जो ONGC जैसी अति भ्रष्ट कम्पनियो की तुलना में ज्यादा अच्छी नजर आये।
.
समाधान -- हमारा प्रस्ताव है कि MRCM , Rtr पेट्रोल मिनिस्टर , Rtr ओएनजीसी चैयरमेन और इन सार्वजनिक उपक्रमों पर ज्यूरी सिस्टम क़ानून लागू किया जाए। MRCM यह सुनिश्चित करेगा कि तेल की रॉयल्टी ऊँचे दाम पर तय हो और यह प्रत्येक भारतीय के खाते में सीधे जमा की जाए। साथ ही राईट टू रिकॉल एवं ज्यूरी सिस्टम इन संस्थाओ में घपले होने की सम्भावना न्यूनतम कर देगा।
.
इन कानूनों के प्रस्तावित ड्राफ्ट देखने के लिए मेरी फेसबुक प्रोफाइल के कवर पेज को क्लिक करे , एवं @pmoindia पर ट्वीट भेजे कि इन कानूनों को गैजेट में प्रकाशित किया जाए।
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Fri Jul 13 2018 10:37:01 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Reliance जिओ and Jio Institure
.
ONGC / GAIL आदि ने तेल की वास्तविक रॉयल्टी से कम चुका कर भारत में बड़े पैमाने पर लूट चलायी और इस घपले से ONGC / GAIL के कर्मचारीयों / मंत्रीयों / जजों ने मोटा पैसा बनाया। ONGC के सामान्य कर्मचारी के पास भी करोड़ो की सम्पत्ति है !! मेरे एक परिचित 2010 में ONGC के शीर्ष अधिकारी के संपर्क में आये थे , और ये अधिकारी तब 300 करोड़ का निवेश करने के लिए प्रॉपर्टी ढूंढ रहे थे !!!
.
तो भारत के सभी सार्वजनिक उपक्रम = PSU संसाधनों को लूटने के अड्डे बने हुए है , और यह लूट मंत्रियो की मिली भगत से चलायी जाती है। और तेल के धंधे में लगे हुए सार्वजनिक उपक्रम इस प्रकार के घपलो के महारथी है।
.
रिलायंस ने भी इस तरह से लूट चलायी। रिलायंस ने मंत्रियो को घूस दी और एवज में मंत्रियो ने उनके लिए रॉयल्टी की सस्ती दरें तय की। और इस तरह रिलायंस ने भारी मुनाफा कमाया। लेकिन रिलायंस के कर्मचारियों के पास घपले करके करोड़ो रूपये बनाने का अवसर नहीं है। हालांकि रिलायंस के शीर्ष अधिकारियों ने इससे हजारो करोड़ रूपये बनाये , किन्तु रिलायंस के मध्यम दर्जे के अधिकारीयों की तुलना में ONGC के कर्मचारी काफी अधिक अमीर है।
.
मुफ्त में लूटे गए इसी पैसे के एक हिस्से का इस्तेमाल जिओ मोबाइल जैसी चैरिटी योजना में किया गया , और जिओ इंस्टीट्यूट भी इसी तरह की चैरिटी योजना है। इस तरह की चैरिटी में व्यक्ति से उसके हिस्से के 100 रूपये नियमो के अधीन लूट लिए जाते है और इसमें से 2 रूपये चैरिटी के रूप में लौटा दिए जाते है। । लेकिन ONGC इस तरह की लूट चलाने के बावजूद इस तरह की कभी कोई चैरिटी नहीं करता। ONGC सिर्फ मंत्रियो एवं कर्मचारियों के लिए ही पैसा बनाता है !!
.
और रिलायंस रोकेफेलर की कम्पनी है। रोकेफेलर सामने से रिलायंस का CEO बनकर इसे नहीं चलाना चाहता, क्योंकि उसे मालूम है कि भारतीयो को गोरो से एलर्जी है। और इसीलिए रोकेफेलर को भारत में कारोबार करने के लिए एक भारतीय चेहरा चाहिए। तो इसीलिए हमारे पास अम्बानी है। 1858 के बाद ब्रिटिश भारतीय राजाओ के माध्यम से ही अपना संचालन करते थे। यह वही स्थिति है।
.
कहानी का सबक यह है कि -- जब तक भारत में ONGC जैसी संस्थाएं लूट चलाती रहेगी तब तक वे ऐसी निजी कंपनियों को खड़ा रखेंगे जो ONGC जैसी अति भ्रष्ट कम्पनियो की तुलना में ज्यादा अच्छी नजर आये।
.
समाधान -- हमारा प्रस्ताव है कि MRCM , Rtr पेट्रोल मिनिस्टर , Rtr ओएनजीसी चैयरमेन और इन सार्वजनिक उपक्रमों पर ज्यूरी सिस्टम क़ानून लागू किया जाए। MRCM यह सुनिश्चित करेगा कि तेल की रॉयल्टी ऊँचे दाम पर तय हो और यह प्रत्येक भारतीय के खाते में सीधे जमा की जाए। साथ ही राईट टू रिकॉल एवं ज्यूरी सिस्टम इन संस्थाओ में घपले होने की सम्भावना न्यूनतम कर देगा।
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sat Jul 14 2018 09:25:02 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

(Q) Can PM print any law, that 50 crore voters of India supported and asked PM to print, and goes against existing laws printed by MPs , without getting new law prinited by LS / RS MPs?
.
My answer : YES. But HOW?
.
PM can first print RTR-SCj-draft i.e. Right to Recall Supreme Court judges as GN. Now RTR-SCj is a GN and so it doesnt need permission of MPs.
.
Now PM can print a GN that 50 crore voters want, without permission of MPs. Now once PM prints a GN. it is law , even if it against laws passed by MPs, unless court cancels the law or court stay orders it. So if 50 crore voters want that law, then judges will NOT dare to cancel that GN printed by PM, after RTR-SCjs-GN has been printed.So Parliament can be bypassed
.
Added later --- any GN printed by PM is "law" and binding on voters unless a court cancels it or stays it. If a voter or an MP thinks that GN is against law printed by MPs, then they will have to ask court to cancel it OR ask MPs to replalce the PM. But till that happens, that GN is binding.
.
So if for whatever reason court refuses to cancel or stay the GN printed by PM, it is binding on voters like all other laws.
.
So Jury System as such will need approval of Parliament. But say 50 crore voters ask PM to print JurySys law, and PM prints it as GN without going to MPs. Then till MPs replace PM (and new PM cancels it) or court cancels JurySys, JurySys start.
.
Now JurySys-law-draft is supported by 50 crore voters, and printed by PM as GN, and NOT approved by MPs, then will SCjs dare to cancel that JurySys GN? If there is law-draft like RTR-SCjs, then imo judges wont dare to cancel any GN supported by 50 crore voters.
.
So if 50 crore voters have asked PM to print a law, then imo we need not worry about what MPs think and do, what judges think and will do etc. We do need to worry about whether desi-videshi elitemen will work to create armed revolt or not.

.
(Q) why did I end SMS to MP campaign method. A campaign method in 2011 was "instruct your MP via SMS" . So why is it now "instruct PM via newindia dot in and postcard"
.
my reaosons ::
.
(1) A large number of our proposals can be implemented by GN, and there is no need for legislation. eg MRCM, RTR-PM, RTR-SCCj are all mere GN that can be printed by PM with no permission from MPs needed. Also, if over 50 crore voters have asked PM to print a GN, that violated existing law printed by MPs, then PM can print the GN and only court can cancel it, And if RTR-SCj law-draft is printed, the courts will never dare to cancel a law-draft supported by 50 crore voters. So MPs can be bypassed by PM as well as voters.
.
(2) Most MPs now clearly and OPENLY say "ask high command and dont bother me, and I will vote as per whip they issue". So we only need to tell PM, and may be leaders of apex parties. So your MP belongs to RSS = BJP, you only need to ask Modiji and no one else. Also, MPs can asked for AADHAR COUNTS of each issues votes for voters in their constituencies. So Pmo can ignore all non-mobile votes, take aadhar# of all votes given by all mobiles, and get pincodes, let loksabha constitiencues for that pincodes and give to MPs
.
(3) Most MPs have defunct mobile numbers of Parliament House website. And newindia keeps and shows LOG of votes casted, SMS are stored in records. So voting on newindia PLUS postcards is better than SMS
.
(4) And One early campaign method was "postcard to PM" (see group - https://fb.com/168513566493928 ) . I left that group because one admin was "Janata Mat" which is a fake profile, and later I decided to end all communications with all fake profiles, unless I personally know that person. But I have always advicated method "postcard to PM" and "postcard to MP". This method was before "SMS to MP". Later in 2013, SMS became cheap, and so "SMS to MP" method was added.
.
(5) And "SMS to PM" was preferred to "SMS to MP", but PM had NOT released any mobile number on net. The Only numbers of PMO were landline and SMS dont reach to such landline number. So "SMS to PM" was better than "SMS to MP", but technically not possible. So "SMS to MP" was started, ALONG with "postcard to PM", and "postcard to MP".
.
(6) Now PM has newindia dot in, which allows voting by mobile and voting by fake email-ids. So vote-count on newindia dot in is partly-useless but partly-useful. But PMO's servers knows which votes came from mobiles and which votes came from fake email ids. So PMO does have accurate voter-count for each proposal. And one of my proposal is to AllowAdharLoginOnly so that count become genuine
.
(7) So count is partly useless , partly useful. So now SMS to MP is less needed if person knows how to use OTP based website. So now voting on newindia is more important than "SMS to MP". Also, SMS of most MPs are NOT on Parliament's website. And most mobiles on Parliament's website are defunct. And SMS has no record, while voting on NewIndia is recorded, and user can take printscreen and post on FB
.

How is economy doing?
.
Manufacturing of may-2018 compared to may-2017 is as follows
.
manufacture of tobacco products = -15.6 % !!
manufacture of wearing apparel = -12.8 % !!
manufacture of textiles = -0.5 %
manufacture of leather and related products = - 1.5 %
manufacture of electrical equipment = -0.6 %
.
manufacture of computer, electronic etc =27.0 %
manufacture of motor vehicles, trailers etc = 21.1 %
.
overall manufacturing = 3% increase only
.
(data from -- govt website , see http://www.mospi.gov.in/sites/default/files/press_release/iip_PR_12july18r.pdf )
.
IOW, goods that all well offs and worse offs consume, be tobacco or textiles or footwear is decreasing
.
And consumptuon of goods that well off cobnsume --- computers, cars etc is rising !!
.
Suffices to say --- the well offs did NOT decrease their of tobaco or apparel etc. So consumption of poor has decreased by more than % amount shown
.
This was expected with GST-law-draft proposed by SoMoKe and congress/rss/aap workers.

प्रश्न : क्या 50 करोड़ मतदाताओं की मांग पर प्रधान संसद की बिना अनुमति से कोई भी कानून बना सकते हैं ?
.
my उत्तर :
हाँ , पर कैसे ?
.
PM can first print Right to Recall Supreme Court judges law-draft. Thats a GN and so doesnt need permission of MPs.
.
ab प्रधान मंत्री कोई भी राष्ट्र स्तर का कानून संदद की बिना अनुमति के कानून गेजेट में छाप और एक बार अगर कोई ड्राफ्ट गेजेट में प्रकाशित हो जाए, to जब तक वो सुप्रीम कोर्ट के जजों द्वारा निरस्त ना कर दिया जाए, tab tak वो एक प्रभावी कानून है । परंतु अगर किसी कानून को 50 करोड़ से ज्यादा नागरिकों का समर्थन है और ये स्थापित है तो किसी जज की हिम्मत नही होगी कि ऐसे कानून का विरोध करें । if any judge cancels, such law supported by 50 crore voters, then voters can expel that judge using RTR SCj procedure. इस तरह अगर किसी ड्राफ्ट पर 50 crore voters' सहमति स्पष्ट है तो प्रधान मंत्री संसद को बाए पास कर सकते हैं ।
.
पर अकेला सांसद कोई भी ड्राफ्ट गेजेट में प्रकाशित नही कर सकता वो सिर्फ ड्राफ्ट को सदन में प्रस्तावित कर सकता है और ये कैफीयत दे सकता है कि ड्राफ्ट प्रस्तावित करना और किसी ड्राफ्ट पर वोट करने हेतु वो पार्टी व्हिप से बाध्य है ।
.
So IMO , if a voter wants a central govt law-draft, then sending message to PM is more important than message to MP. In fact, imo, sending message to PM will suffice. The message can via postcard, newindia vote and twitter, all three.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Jul 15 2018 13:32:49 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

(Q) Can PM print any law, that 50 crore voters of India supported and asked PM to print, without permission of LS / RS MPs?
.
my answer :
.
YES. But HOW?
.
Any GN (GN = Gazette Notification) printed by PM is a law on citizens till a court cancels it, The officers who implement law take orders from PM and judges, and not from MPs.
.
So if for whatever reason court refuses to cancel or stay the GN printed by PM, it is binding on voters like all other laws.
.
So PM can first print RTR-SCj-draft i.e. Right to Recall Supreme Court judges as GN. Now thats a GN and doesnt need permission of MPs.
.
Now PM can print a GN that 50 crore voters want, without permission of MPs. Now once PM prints a GN. it is law , even if it against laws passed by MPs, unless court cancels the law or court stay orders it. So if 50 crore voters want that law, then judges will NOT dare to cancel that GN printed by PM, after RTR-SCjs-GN has been printed.So Parliament can be bypassed
.
Consider an example. Say Jury System law-draft. As such, this needs to be passed by Parliament. But say 50 crore voters ask PM to print JurySys law, and PM prints it as GN without first asking MPs to print the law. Then till MPs replace PM (and new PM cancels it) or court cancels JurySys, JurySys start.
.
Now JurySys-law-draft is supported by 50 crore voters, and printed by PM as GN, and NOT approved by MPs, then will SCjs dare to cancel that JurySys GN? If there is law-draft like RTR-SCjs, then imo judges wont dare to cancel any GN supported by 50 crore voters. And if judge still dares, then voters can expel the SCj using Right to Recall Supreme judges GN and next judge can cancel previous judges' order, and that would restore JurySys.
.
So if 50 crore voters have asked PM to print a law, then imo we need not worry about what MPs think and do, what judges think and will do etc. We do need to worry about whether desi-videshi elitemen will work to create armed revolt or not.

Should we ask @PmoIndia to print a law or MP or both ; and is asking ONLY @PmoIndia "bad"?
.
My answer :
.
(a) communicating to @PmoIndia is must. Method I propose to communicate is @PmoIndia are sending postcard, voting on newindia and tweeting @PmoIndia .
.
(b) after that, sending postcard to MP may be sufficient. One may send SMS, tweet to MP, but after sending msg to PM, I dont see that necessary
.
WHY?
.
(1) Most laws we have proposed are GNs (GN = Gazette Notifications) to be printed by PM. So MPs' permission isnt needed at all
.
(2) If over 50 crore voters have asked PM to print a law, and even when Parliament's permission is technically needed, it is politically feasible for PM to bypass MPs. (see fb.com/10155421147466922 )
.
So imo, communicating to @PmoIndia is sufficient. After that, one may communicate to MP via postcard, SMS, tweeter etc. But imo, now it isnt that important
.
------------------
.
The three main purposes of communicating to PM / MP are
.
(1) purpose-1 : seeing whether supporter is fake or real -- say a person says he supports MRCM, Say he refuses to ask PM / MP to print MRCM. That that convinces us activists that that activist is a fake. IOW, primary purpose is to differentiate between real and fake. And so if a person has communicated to PM via newindia dot in, postcard and tweet, then he has already showed that he is NOT fake. So we need NOT ask him to prove again, by asking him to tweet MP (on his personal handle) and so on.
.
(2) purpose-2 : creating a count before PM / MPs --- by sending postcards to PM, voting on newindia, and tweeting to @PmoIndia , he has created count in PMO. When count in PmoIndia reaches in crores, the MP will come to know thru their unofficial communication channels. And when count is in few hundreds, creating count at one place like Pmo is sufficient.
.
(3) purpose-3 : convincing all and convincing Udham Singh that count is N --- this is needed ONLY after count is in crores. eg today, less than 10000 voters across India support MRCM. So we get NOTHING by telling 90 crore voters and telling Udham Singh that "see, 10000 voters in India support MRCM !!" Because count is so low, the Udham Singh cant act. When crores of voters have communicated to Pmo, ONLY after that convincing crores and convincing Udham Singh that count has crossed crores will be necessary
,.
--------------
.
So communication to @PmoIndia via postcard, tweet and newindia is PRIMARY. Communication to MP is secondary, and I have taken it as optional.\
.
---------------
.
So finally --- I will restate the question -- should we ask @PmoIndia to print a law or or should we ask MP or should we ask both ; and is asking ONLY @PmoIndia "bad"?
.
My answer is : If a person has asked MP, but refuses to ask @pmoIndia via ALL three channels (postcard, newindia and tweet) then I will dismiss him as fake and walk away. Asking MP is a plus, but I wont insist it as condition to decide real or fake.

.
In year may-2009 , I had suggested that giving cheap Rs 1000 mobile phone free with 100 minutes of free outgoing talktime can reduce to ALL can reduce naxal problem and poverty across India. I had taken it from Advanaji''s manifesto, and its also common sense.
.
But after election, all congress/rss/aap leaders including SoMoKe and all congress/rss/aap workers decided to ignore this scheme
.
Well, now Chhatisgadh govt has shown some wisdom, and working on this plan.
.
Now imagine how many lives, time etc could have been saved if this was done in year 2009. But cold fact is that SoMoKe and congress/rss/aap leaders want to keep poor poor and un-connected so that they can be looted with ease. And so they opposed the scheme to give free mobile to all. Now that things are going out of hand, they are left with no option but to do this charity
.
This has been The Naxal Argument since ages that "we naxals may fail to "win" because govt may give rice, mobiles. etc. But if we had never raised arms, then would India's leaders and political workers bothered to provide rice or mobile to tribals?. And if they had provided rice and mobile on day one, would tribals had bothered to joined our groups?".
.
Giving free mobile and some 200 minute of free talktime and some 1 GB of free data is welcome. And it will reduce poverty and also reduce naxal. And for better reduction, I request ALL to support MRCM law-draft, As per MRCM-law-draft, the mineral royalties will be directly deposited in accounts of citizens. .

.

.

So now if A gives name of a TRUE activist to C, then movement will benefit. But if A ends up giving name of a fake activist, then movement will get damaged. So every activist A will need list of true activist B
.

.
(1) ask B vote at https://newindia.in/causes/DdMrcam , and check on his mobile that B has already voted
.
(2) ask B to send postcard to Pm with only few lines, like "sub : DDMRCAM , pls pint https://newindia.in/causes/DdMrcam law-draft - name, voterid" and signature as optional
.
(3) ask B to tweet "@PmoIndia #DdMrcam https://newindia.in/causes/DdMrcam ".
.
If B doesnt take these 3 steps, then A can presume that B is timepass. And imo, if A spends more time with B, then A will be wasting his time.
.
Utilizing time properly is very essential in activism.

(Q) Can PM print any law, that 50 crore voters of India supported and asked PM to print, without permission of LS / RS MPs?
.
my answer :
.
YES. But HOW?
.
Any GN (GN = Gazette Notification) printed by PM is a law on citizens till a court cancels it, The officers who implement law take orders from PM and judges, and not from MPs.
.
So if for whatever reason court refuses to cancel or stay the GN printed by PM, it is binding on voters like all other laws.
.
So PM can first print RTR-SCj-draft i.e. Right to Recall Supreme Court judges as GN. Now thats a GN and doesnt need permission of MPs.
.
Now PM can print a GN that 50 crore voters want, without permission of MPs. Now once PM prints a GN. it is law , even if it against laws passed by MPs, unless court cancels the law or court stay orders it. So if 50 crore voters want that law, then judges will NOT dare to cancel that GN printed by PM, after RTR-SCjs-GN has been printed.So Parliament can be bypassed
.
Consider an example. Say Jury System law-draft. As such, this needs to be passed by Parliament. But say 50 crore voters ask PM to print JurySys law, and PM prints it as GN without first asking MPs to print the law. Then till MPs replace PM (and new PM cancels it) or court cancels JurySys, JurySys start.
.
Now JurySys-law-draft is supported by 50 crore voters, and printed by PM as GN, and NOT approved by MPs, then will SCjs dare to cancel that JurySys GN? If there is law-draft like RTR-SCjs, then imo judges wont dare to cancel any GN supported by 50 crore voters. And if judge still dares, then voters can expel the SCj using Right to Recall Supreme judges GN and next judge can cancel previous judges' order, and that would restore JurySys.
.
So if 50 crore voters have asked PM to print a law, then imo we need not worry about what MPs think and do, what judges think and will do etc. We do need to worry about whether desi-videshi elitemen will work to create armed revolt or not.

Will PM ever say "I passed such and such law because so many real / fake people voted on XYZ website"?
.
Sometimes, one needs to apply mind before accepting or even making claims, and test such claims against political reality.
.

If voters start making demands before PM, then it will become difficult for PM to evade the demand. And so from day one, PM and sponsors work to create an illusion that "PM is tough, and PM will never listen to what masses say".
.
Thats why, whatever law PMs have printed, they have always used "mass welfare" as pretext and never used "public demand": as pretext.
.
So no PM, unless laws like RTR PM come, will ever admit that "I printed such and such law because N voters wrote postcard to me, or N voters asked me via some website, or N voters tweeted me or N voters had take out rally !!", If at all he admits, then next day onwards, all autocracy of PM will be over and voters will start pressurizing him to print 10s of laws that voters want
.
So some statements (NOT MY STATEMENTS) go as
,.
(1) people should NOT write postcards to PM, because usuk-elitemen can hire agents to write crores of postcards using voters names and voterids, and then PM can pass these ;laws by saying "see, I got 1 crore postcard to make this law". PM will be exteremely stupid to say so. If he does, then next day, crores of REAL voters will write postcards and that would force PM to make laws voters want
.
(2) people should NOT take out rallies because usuk-elitemen can hire agents to lakhs and crores of workers , and motivate them via paidmedia, to take out rallies and PM can print law by citing them as reason. PM will be exteremely stupid to say that "I printed this law because of rallies" , because If he does, then next day, crores of REAL voters will write postcards and that would force PM to make laws voters want
.
So last thing any PM in India would do is "praise or blame voters to make any law", because then next day, voters force on PM will go ten times
.
So one can all these nonsense that "PM will do this and blame voters and PM will do that and he will blame voters" are nonsense. Today also, PM does whatever he can, and in the end, all blame comes on voters only "see, after all, you had voted for this PM".
.
So PM and many will always blame voters for all wrong action,. But question is --- how many accept their nonsense logic. Not even their supporters.
.
For that matter, congress/rss/aap workers have always opposed RTR on this argument that "if RTR comes, then paidmediamen will create public opinion against a good leader and get him expelled and so there should be no RTR" !! The argument that "PM will use a votecount of a wbeiste, that everyone knows allows fake voting, to call it as public voice", is ludicurous as best. PMs of India have been defunct, but there is a limit below which they can stoop. So no PM can use votecount of a website that allows fakevoting to justify his action.
.
So why is voting on newindia and writing postcard to PM or sending tweet to @PmoIndia is needed? Its ONLY to separate real supporter from fake. If YOU meet a person, and claims he support law-draft-X (that you also support), you can ask him to vote on newindia or send postcard or send tweet. And when he refuses, you know that he is fake
.
eg I run into many congress/rss/aap workers who claim that they support RTR. I ask them to write postcard to PM, and immidiately they refuse. That proves that they in reality dont support any RTR-law-draft, and they are ONLY fakes. This saves my time.
.
So imo activists need not worry that PM would blame voters, All political party workers blame voters anyway, and they will keep blaming, and no one believes them,

Proofs that I supported some law-drafts on newindia dot in
.
Newindia dot in gives a list to citizen himself ONLY (not third party) which law-drafts he has supported, The citizen can upload these screenshots on FB, and thus give a "semi-reliable" proof that he did support these law-drafts. The proof is semi reliable, because one can forge the JPG files !!
.

.
If a person has NOT supported a law-draft on newindia dot in, and if that person claims that he is supporter of that law-draft, and then I request ALL to dismiss him as a fake, and not waste much time with him. Time is more than money. Time is life.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Jul 16 2018 14:09:23 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Those who filed income tax returns early may get notice from income tax dept !!!
.
WHY?
,
Because many PSU as well as private banks have filed TDS and interested earned between 01-apr-2017 to 31-mar-2018 information as late as 02-july-2018 !! And so they will get reflected in person's so called 26as on 09-july-2018. So if that person filed return based on a 26as which he had downloaded before 08-july-2018, then the interest earned data he gave will mismatch with final data given by the bank !!!
.
And so a notice will be sent to him !!
.
On one hand, income tax dept sent be 10 emails asking me to file return asap. And on other hand, banks are given interest earned data and TDS data on as late as 02-jul-2018, which becomes available to person on 09-july-2018 !!
.
And notice may result allegation of under-reporting income and a scrutiny as well !!
.
GoI should first force PSU banks to ensure that all TDS and interest earned information is filed before 15-apr-2018. And update all 26as within a wek. And only then ask persons to file income tax returns !!

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Jul 16 2018 14:28:47 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

If any recallist is making too many phone calls to you or tagging you again and again etc, then pls note clearly --- PESTERING is against my policy , and I wont mind at all if you block him
.
Too many recallists complaint to me that some ABC or XYZ recallist phonecalls them again and again. And he sends messages on fb / whatsapp again and again, or tags him again and again or repeatedly mentions his name in comment so that a notification comes. And he is make repeated request to share or give opinions. It is called PESTERING.
.
Well, I am COMPLETELY against pestering as a campaign tool.
.
First, I only believe in PUBLIC communication via FB, and if there is any imp information, then I msg via FB / WA , but seldom phonecall. And imo phonecall should be used rarely.
.
eg I put law-draft with summary, links etc on FB, and make public request to ALL in my status post to read the draft, summaries etc. But I never phonecall them or tag them or put their names in comments to send notifications etc. i.e. I do NOT pester them in any way to read the draft or give opinion or vote. If there is very important instruction, then I send them via FB message ONCE , and only once.
.
I support communication by "2 person thread" or "2 person album".
.
So if anyone is buggering you again and again, by phonecalls, pls note --- I never asked anyone to campaign like that
.
And many recallists tell me that XYZ calls me too often, and they ask me to ask XYZ not to call so often. Well, I will NOT ask anyone not to call, just as I never asked anyone to call. You pls ask him not to call, or block him.
.
---
.
And in comments here, please DO NOT write names of anyone who bugs you. This post is ONLY to send you a general message, and NOT to collect complaints. ...

.

.

.

.

.

.
https://twitter.com/MehtaRahulC/status/1019535758472941568
.
I have put this tweet via SMS to show that even those who dont have smart phone can tweet to @ PmoIndia and request him print #lawdrafts
.
----
.
This is done by going into twitter -> settings and putting users' mobile ph#. This onetime, After that, send SMS to 9248948837 = 92489-48837 , and tweet will come.
.
----
.
This was main reason , why I no longer support my 2011 campaign method of "send instruction to MP via SMS". Because if one can send PUBLIC tweet to @PmoIndia (and also to @MP) via twitter, then imo there is NO use of sending SMS to MP. SMS leaves no record, and isnt public. While SMS to twitter server 9248948837 = 92489-48837 becomes public and visible to all via twitter.
.
----
.
So activist will need to meet a voter, open a twitter account for the voter, link voter's twitter account with voter's dumbphone or smartphone. Thats one time , per voter. Once link is established, voter can send SMS to 92489-48837 , and his tweet will come on twitter.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Wed Jul 18 2018 16:37:15 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

देश मे FDI के वजह से Repatriation crisis कैसे हो सकते है यह एक साधारण उदाहरण से समझते है।
.
Repatriation मतलब FDI के जरिये जो डॉलर देश मे आएगा फिर उस पर जो विदेशी कंपनी द्वारा मुनाफा कमाया जाता है,
वह मुनाफा वापस डॉलर मे ही देना पड़ता है। Repatriation के वजह से देश की अर्थव्यवस्था एक दिन मे बैठ जाती है ।
.
कल्पना करो देश के महान नेता FDI फास्ट फूड मे डॉलर का निवेश लाते है, --- MacDonald Company, KFC इत्यादी।
.
तो कल्पना करिए MacDonald जैसी फास्ट फूड बेचने वाली कम्पनी फ़ास्ट फूड क्षेत्र मे मात्र 1 डॉलर का निवेश करती है। यह 1 डॉलर लेकर MacDonald कम्पनी RBI के पास जाती है। तो RBI, MacDonald को आज के हिसाब से 1 डॉलर के बदले 60 रुपये देती है। फिर यह 60 रुपये लेकर MacDonald बाजार मे आती है। (Note - अब RBI के पास सिर्फ 1 ही डॉलर है)
.
फिर यह कम्पनी बाजार से 60 रुपये मे कुछ आधा किलो आलू, एक पाव तेल, पाव की लादी, और पकाने हेतु गॅस खरीद लाती है।
.
आधा किलो आलू 10 रुपये
एक पाव तेल 20 रुपये
एक पाव की लादी 10 रुपये
LPG गॅस 15 रुपये की लग गयी
.
फिर भी 5 रुपये बच गए, जो की कोई नौकर रखा, तो उसको दिये (इतनी रोजगारी बढ़ी)।
.
फिर ऊपर दिये सामग्री मे 10 वडापाव तैयार हुए
.
अब देखा जाए तो एक वडापाव ज्यादा से ज्यादा 10 रुपये का होता है। पर MacDonald कम्पनी उसे 15 रुपये मे बेचती है।
.
तो 10 वडापाव के हुए (10*15)= 150 रुपये।
.
अब 150 रुपये मे से 60 रुपये का उसका निवेश हटा देते है
(150 - 60)= 90 रुपये बचते है।
.
90 रुपये MacDonald कम्पनी का शुद्ध रूपए से मुनाफा है, जो के कानून उनको उनके देश मे ले जाना चाहिए (आखिर सब लोग मुनाफा कमाने हेतु ही तो यहा आते है)।
.
पर यह 90 रुपये वह उनके देश मे ले नहीं जा सकते, क्योकि भारत के रुपये उनके देश मे किस काम के?
.
तो MacDonald यह 90 रुपये लेकर पहुंच जाता है RBI के पास और RBI से 340 बदले डॉलर की मांग करता है।

तो अब RBI को आज के हिसाब से (90/60)= 1.5 डॉलर देने पड़ेगे। पर यहा पर थोड़ी परेशानी है। पहले मैने कहा था के RBI के पास डॉलर नहीं है। FDI in Fast Food के जरिये RBI के पास आज की तारीख मे सिर्फ 1 डॉलर है।
.
और MacDonald को तो अब 1.5 डॉलर देने है। तो यह बाकी बचे 0.50 डॉलर अब RBI कैसे देती है ?
.
तो RBI/ सरकार को झक मारकर World Bank से 0.50 डॉलर का कर्जा लेना पड़ता है। यह पर थोड़ी दिक्कत है World Bank किसी भी देश को ऐसे ही कर्जा नहीं देती, कुछ शर्तो पर कर्जा मिलता है।जैसे
.
1) जिस देश को कर्जा चाहिए उस देश को अपने रुपये की कीमत को डॉलर की तुलना मे घटाना पड़ता है।
.
2) या तो फिर उस देश को अपने Natural Resources को World Bank के पास गिरवी रखना पड़ता है।
.
3)या तो फिर आप अपने देश मे और दुसरी विदेशी कंपनी को अपने देश मे व्यापार करने का license दो।
.
वगेरा वगेरा इसी तरह और भी कई होती है
.
यह सारी शर्ते मानकर RBI अब World Bank से 0.50 डॉलर का कर्जा लेती है। अब यह कर्जा कोई सरकार नहीं देश की जनता को चुकाना पड़ता है। साथ मे डॉलर की तुलना मे रुपये की कीमत गिरने की वजह से देश की अर्थव्यवस्था एक ही दिन मे बैठ जाती है।
.
इससे ज्यादा भयावह कहानी दूसरे क्षेत्रो मे होती है। यह तो साधारण उदाहरण से हम ने समझा, बाकी की परिस्थिति की आप लोग कल्पना करे।

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Thu Jul 19 2018 03:29:54 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Re : suggestion that "allow only those who voted to participate in recall process" in RTR-MP-law-draft. Imo, a useless suggestion.
.
In the RTR-law-draft I have proposed, one suggestion that I got and I dismiss as useless, is -- in recall process, only those who voted in initial election should be allowed to participate.
.
So as per this useless suggestion, say there 20 lakh voters in Loksabha, and say 15 lakh voted, then in recall process, which may or may not occur, only these 15 lakh should be allowed to participate.
.
Here are reasons why I dismiss this as nonsense, Following are my reasons.
.
(1) The people who made this proposal are yet to give complete RTR-MP-law-draft that adds this suggestion. I have general tendency to dismiss all draftless wonderful proposed laws as useless. So this is one reason why I have dismissed this as useless suggestion.
.
(2) A voter didnt vote because he saw all candidates worthless and he also saw NOTA as worthless. But in recall process, a better option has come, and so now he is willing to participate for recall process. So I am against blocking that voter.
.
(3) Each booth officer keeps record of who voted on paper. And these papers are destroyed after 90 days unless there is a court case and court order. So to implement "allow only those who voted to recall", ECI will need to keep this paper record for 5 years and will also need to make xerox copies and may also need to enter it electronically !! Imo, its a HUGE useless expenditure.
.
(4) Say candidate-C or supporter of C knows that voter-V is likely to recall C if C wins election. And there is voter-S who supports C, but is unable to vote on that day. So chances are high that C will bribe booth officer to mark "S voted and V didnt vote" !!! To avoid that, one would need to ask booth officer to give receipt to every voter who voted, and collector staff or ECI staff will need preserve copy of that receipt for 5 years !! And a whole layer of security will be needed to ensure that booth officers keep correct record of those who voted and those who didnt. So again, costs shoots up, And there will 1000s of complaints and whole layer needed to resolve them
.
(5) Finally, a voter could not vote because he didnt get voting card even though he had applied. But now he has card. I see no reason to exclude him in recall process now. If he is to be included as per this proposal, then another layer of record keeping is needed. And imo, its another huge useless expense.
.
(6) A voter didnt vote because he was below 18 years of age on voting date. But now he is eligible. I see no reason to stop him from participating in recall process. Does this proposal include him? I cant say without seeing the draft, and the proposal maker doesnt give draft !!
.
(7) Say candidate-C knows that voter-V is likely to recall C if C wins election. So chances are now HIGHER that C will use force to stop V from voting. So again, costs of policing election increases
.
And I will write more after those who want this feature give DRAFT of implementing the restriction they have proposed.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Fri Jul 20 2018 05:04:28 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

What is repatriation and what is "repatriation" crisis? How FDI and debt may BOTH worsen repatriation crisis? solution?
.
List of chapters
--------------------
.
(1) what is repatriation commitment?
(2) do we have dollars to give back?
(3) total potential repatriation commitment on RBI (approx)
(4) what is repatriation crisis?
(5) what are the effects are repatriation crisis?
(6) solution?
.
======
.
chap-1 : what is repatriation commitment?
.
(1.1) Repatriation commitment is unwritten promise that RBI has given to those who give dollars and get rupees, that when they give rupees back, RBI shall give them dollars as per exchange rate of that day
.
(1.2) Say a company such as say McDonalds wants to do business in India. To do business, it will need to buy / rent land, pay salaries, pay advertisement companies, buy raw materials etc And it will generate sales and may generate profits or suffer losses
.
(1.3) Now to buy / rent land, it needs rupees. But McDonald has only dollars. So McDonald will give say $ 1 crore to RBI (or agency authorized by RBI like say SBI etc) and in turn, RBI will give say Rs 65 crores (say exchange rate is $ 1 = Rs 70) .
.
(1.4) Repatriation commitment on capital --- now an unwritten commitment RBI has given, which RBI has NOT put on net (and you will need to ask CA to verify) is that whenever McDonald gives rupees worth $ 1 crore back to RBI, RBI is "required" to give $ 1 crore to McDonald
.
(1.5) Repatriation commitment on profits --- lets say McDonald earns Rs 20 crore , after taxes, from operations, And it pays Rs 20 crore to RBI.And say exchange rate that say is Rs 65. Then RBI is "required" to give Rs 20 crore / 65 = $ 30.80 lakhs to McDonald.
.
(1.6) Repatriation commitment on capital gains -- lets say McDonald had bought some land for say Rs 5 crore and later sold it for Rs 7 crore. Then Rs 2 crore is capital gains, after taxes. This capital gains is conceptually similar to profit, but legally different. So again, if McDonald gives this capital gains of Rs 2 crore to RBI , then RBI is required
.
So lets add (1.4) , (1.5), (1.6) and subtract losses
.
Say McDonald bought $ 1 crore , and exchange rate was Rs 60 and so it got Rs 60 crores from RBI. It invested that, and after 5 years ,
....... accumlated profits after taxes = say Rs 10 crore
........ capital gains = Rs 15 crore .
say exchange rate = Rs 70
.
Then repatriation commitment on RBI = (Rs 65 crore + Rs 10 crore + Rs 15 crore ) / 70 = Rs 90 crore / 70 = $ 1.29 crore approx
.
i.e. if McDonald were to sell all its assets and decide to wind up, and pay Rs 90 crore to RBI, then RBI as per its unwritten repatriation promise has to give $1.29 crores.
.
But if McDonald had made loses of say Rs 15 crore and capital losses of say Rs 20 crore, then repatriation commitment would be = (Rs 65 crore - Rs 15 crore - Rs 20 crore ) / 70 = Rs 30 crore / 70 = $ 0.43 crore approx
.
So RBI's unwritten commitment to repay dollars INCREASES if foreign company makes profits or capital gains. And repatriation commitment decreases if company makes losses of capital losses.
.
So in case of debt, repayment amount in dollars is fixed --- capital plus interest. But in case of FDI, the repatriation amount depends on profits / losses and capital gains / losses. It may be much much higher than debt, may be less
.
Now FII , FPI etc also create repatriation commitment in same way
.
====================
.
chap-2 : Do we have dollars to give back even initial capital?
.
Now say McDonald doesnt make any profit or losses. and it asks for $ 1 crore back. Then also, will Govt of India be able to repay $ 1 crore ?
.
The answer is NO.. WHY NOT?
.
Because every year, our imports exceed (exports + remittance) . Remittance is when person living in Gulf or USA sends dollars to his own account in India or his relatives' account in India. In case of remittance, govt takes dollars, gives rupees and there is NO repatriation commitment.
.
Why are our imports so high? Well, imo, usuk-elitemen have ensured that leaders dont improve public transport, and make car / scooter buying easier and easier, and leaders destroy footpaths. So card / scooter sale increases and so petrol consumption further rises. And so huge amount of forex is consumed by crude oil import. In addition, govt has kept import duty low. And local manufacturing is poor. So exports are low and imports are high.
.
so consider gap = imports - exports - remittance
.
This gap is covered by net FDI inflow and net debt intake
.
Now for India, for past several years, the imports are so higher than exports, i.e. the gap is so high, that much of the FDI and debt is consumed by this gap.
.
So say McDonald doesnt make any profit or losses. and it asks for only $ 1 crore , that it had given, back. Then also, will Govt of India be able to repay $ 1 crore ?
.
Answer is NO. Because that $ 1 crore would get consumed in importing crude oil etc
.
So whatever dollars that are coming via FDI / debt, get consumed by gap between imports and exports
.
===================
.
(3) total potential repatriation commitment on RBI (approx)
.
Here are some approximate numbers, as in march-2018 (see RBI doc link in comment)
.
(approx numbers)
.
total forex reserves of RBI = $ 430 billion
India's investment in other countries = $ 200 billion
TOTAL India's investment in foreign countries = $ 630 billion
.
total foreign debt = $ 513 billion
total FDI , including unrepatriated profits = $ 400 billion
total other foreign investment (FII, FPI, credits) = $ 200 billion
TOTAL foreign investments into India = $ 1050 billion
.
So if India has to repay debt, and also fulfill all the FDI repatriation and other repatriation commitment, even after paying away entire reserves, there will be short fall of about $ 400.billion !!
.
And that is assuming that India can get dollars back for $ 200 billion it has invested outside India !! That is shaky assumption, because some of the investment is in developing economies like Nepal, Bhutan, Bangladeshi, African countries etc who dont have dollars to repay. So as such, dollar shortfall is much higher than $ 400 billion, it would be above $ 500 billion.
.
=================
.
(4) What is repatriation crisis?
.
Repatriation crisis, is when country runs of out of dollars to repay those to whom it has given commitment to repay.
.
So how does FDI worsen repatriation crisis?
.
As of today in India. the forex that comes via FDI gets consumed in importing crude oil, electrinics etc. So there is no forex left even to repay the capital, if the FDI-bringing-company wants its forex back. Next, FDI-brining companies have superior technology and processes, and so they in general generate more profits. The profit after taxes is repatriable.. So TOTAL POTENTIAL repatriation burden on India increases.
.
So FDI, like FII or debt, increases the possibility of repatriation crisis.
.
=============
.
So what are the effects are repatriation crisis?
And what are the solutions?
.
I will put in next posts in this album(incomplete --- comments posted will be deleted)

step - 01: using English for NAME on FB profile
.
step - 01 : pls put first two or all words in the NAME on your FB profile in ENGLISH. And in case you want to use hindi or local language, then you may put first two letters of your name in English and next two words in name in local language.
.
---
.
my reason in short : FB search doesnt work well when name is NOT in English
.
my explanations in detail
.
(1) FB search doesnt work well when your name is in non-English language such as Hindi, Gujarati etc.

(2) So say an activist who just knows your name wants to reach to your profile to say read law-drafts you have proposed or to communicate to you.
.
(3) Then most common way and sometimes only way that activist can use to reach your profile, is by using SEARCH feature of FB
.
(4) so if your name is written in English, then that activist will be able to see your profile in search result and read your posts etc. Otherwise, he will end up spending a lot of time in just searching for your profile and may give up
.
So pls keep the NAME of FB profile in English

.

.

.

.
my REASONS , why an activist should not allow others to put post on his wall, and should not allow others to TAG :
.
(1) If an activist doesnt remove tags others created or an activist allows others to write posts on his wall , then his wall will become full of 10s of posts that have nothing to do with law-drafts and campaign methods that that activist is promoting.
.
(2) Those putting posts on that activist's wall or those tagging him may put public posts or tag him to public posts. Then that post will come in newsfeed of all who are following him. Now many persons who are following the activist on FB are following him for his political posts,. And so that activists' posts on law-drafts and campaign methods will also not come on their newsfeeds.
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate
